<TABLE STYLE="BORDER-COLLAPSE:COLLAPSE" BORDER="0" CELLPADDING="0" CELLSPACING="0" WIDTH="100%">
<TR>
<TD WIDTH="9%" VALIGN="top" ALIGN="left"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2"><B>Item&nbsp;1.01</B></FONT></TD>
<TD ALIGN="left" VALIGN="top"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2"><B>Entry into a Material Definitive Agreement. </B></FONT></TD></TR></TABLE> <P STYLE="font-size:6px;margin-top:0px;margin-bottom:0px">&nbsp;</P>
<TABLE STYLE="BORDER-COLLAPSE:COLLAPSE" BORDER="0" CELLPADDING="0" CELLSPACING="0" WIDTH="100%">
<TR>
<TD WIDTH="9%" VALIGN="top" ALIGN="left"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2"><B>Item&nbsp;8.01</B></FONT></TD>
<TD ALIGN="left" VALIGN="top"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2"><B>Other Events. </B></FONT></TD></TR></TABLE> <P STYLE="margin-top:6px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">On
November&nbsp;11, 2009, Advanced Micro Devices, Inc. (&#147;<B><I>AMD</I></B>&#148; or the &#147;<B><I>Company</I></B>&#148;) entered into a Settlement Agreement (the &#147;<B><I>Settlement Agreement</I></B>&#148;) and a Patent Cross License
Agreement (the &#147;<B><I>Cross License Agreement</I></B>&#148;) with Intel Corporation (&#147;<B><I>Intel</I></B>&#148;). </FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:18px;margin-bottom:0px"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2"><B><I>Settlement
Agreement </I></B></FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:6px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">The Settlement Agreement provides that Intel will pay $1.25 billion to the Company within 30 days of
execution of the Settlement Agreement. The Settlement Agreement also includes mutual releases of all claims between the Company and Intel, including specified claims under the existing Cross License Agreements between the parties. With respect to
claims regarding Intel&#146;s business practices, the Company released all claims through the date of the Settlement Agreement. The Company has also agreed to dismiss with prejudice its actions against Intel currently pending in Delaware and Japan
and to withdraw all of its regulatory complaints against Intel worldwide. </FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">Pursuant to the Settlement Agreement, Intel has
agreed to abide by a set of business practice provisions. These provisions prohibit Intel from, among other things, with respect to the microprocessor market: </FONT></P> <P STYLE="font-size:6px;margin-top:0px;margin-bottom:0px">&nbsp;</P>
<TABLE STYLE="BORDER-COLLAPSE:COLLAPSE" BORDER="0" CELLPADDING="0" CELLSPACING="0" WIDTH="100%">
<TR>
<TD WIDTH="4%"><FONT SIZE="1">&nbsp;</FONT></TD>
<TD WIDTH="4%" VALIGN="top" ALIGN="left"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">a)</FONT></TD>
<TD ALIGN="left" VALIGN="top"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">Offering inducements to customers in exchange for their agreement to buy all of their microprocessor needs from Intel, whether on a geographic, a market segment, a
product segment or distribution channel basis; </FONT></TD></TR></TABLE> <P STYLE="font-size:6px;margin-top:0px;margin-bottom:0px">&nbsp;</P>
<TABLE STYLE="BORDER-COLLAPSE:COLLAPSE" BORDER="0" CELLPADDING="0" CELLSPACING="0" WIDTH="100%">
<TR>
<TD WIDTH="4%"><FONT SIZE="1">&nbsp;</FONT></TD>
<TD WIDTH="4%" VALIGN="top" ALIGN="left"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">b)</FONT></TD>
<TD ALIGN="left" VALIGN="top"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">Offering inducements to customers in exchange for their agreement to limit or delay their purchase of microprocessors from AMD, whether on a geographic, a market
segment or any other basis; </FONT></TD></TR></TABLE> <P STYLE="font-size:6px;margin-top:0px;margin-bottom:0px">&nbsp;</P>
<TABLE STYLE="BORDER-COLLAPSE:COLLAPSE" BORDER="0" CELLPADDING="0" CELLSPACING="0" WIDTH="100%">
<TR>
<TD WIDTH="4%"><FONT SIZE="1">&nbsp;</FONT></TD>
<TD WIDTH="4%" VALIGN="top" ALIGN="left"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">c)</FONT></TD>
<TD ALIGN="left" VALIGN="top"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">Offering inducements to customers in exchange for their agreement to limit their promotion, production or distribution of products containing AMD microprocessors;
</FONT></TD></TR></TABLE> <P STYLE="font-size:6px;margin-top:0px;margin-bottom:0px">&nbsp;</P>
<TABLE STYLE="BORDER-COLLAPSE:COLLAPSE" BORDER="0" CELLPADDING="0" CELLSPACING="0" WIDTH="100%">
<TR>
<TD WIDTH="4%"><FONT SIZE="1">&nbsp;</FONT></TD>
<TD WIDTH="4%" VALIGN="top" ALIGN="left"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">d)</FONT></TD>
<TD ALIGN="left" VALIGN="top"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">Offering inducements to customers in exchange for their agreement to abstain from or delay their participation in AMD product launches, announcements, advertising or
other promotional activities; </FONT></TD></TR></TABLE> <P STYLE="font-size:6px;margin-top:0px;margin-bottom:0px">&nbsp;</P>
<TABLE STYLE="BORDER-COLLAPSE:COLLAPSE" BORDER="0" CELLPADDING="0" CELLSPACING="0" WIDTH="100%">
<TR>
<TD WIDTH="4%"><FONT SIZE="1">&nbsp;</FONT></TD>
<TD WIDTH="4%" VALIGN="top" ALIGN="left"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">e)</FONT></TD>
<TD ALIGN="left" VALIGN="top"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">Offering inducements to retailers or distributors to limit or delay their purchase or distribution of computer systems or platforms containing AMD microprocessors; and
</FONT></TD></TR></TABLE> <P STYLE="font-size:6px;margin-top:0px;margin-bottom:0px">&nbsp;</P>
<TABLE STYLE="BORDER-COLLAPSE:COLLAPSE" BORDER="0" CELLPADDING="0" CELLSPACING="0" WIDTH="100%">
<TR>
<TD WIDTH="4%"><FONT SIZE="1">&nbsp;</FONT></TD>
<TD WIDTH="4%" VALIGN="top" ALIGN="left"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">f)</FONT></TD>
<TD ALIGN="left" VALIGN="top"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">Withholding any benefit or threatening retaliation against anyone for their refusal to enter into a prohibited arrangement such as those set forth above.
</FONT></TD></TR></TABLE>

<p Style='page-break-before:always'>
<HR  SIZE="3" style="COLOR:#999999" WIDTH="100%" ALIGN="CENTER">

 <P STYLE="margin-top:0px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">The Settlement Agreement terminates after 10 years from the date of the Settlement
Agreement. The business practice restrictions set forth above terminate upon the earliest of (a)&nbsp;10 years from the date of the Settlement Agreement, (b)&nbsp;the date upon which Mercury Research reports that Intel has less than a 65% market
share in the Worldwide PC Market Segment for four consecutive quarters and (c)&nbsp;any attempt by the Company to transfer its rights or obligations under the Settlement Agreement, except as expressly provided in the Settlement Agreement.
</FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:18px;margin-bottom:0px"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2"><B><I>Cross License Agreement </I></B></FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:6px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT
STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">Under the Cross License Agreement, Intel has granted to the Company and the Company&#146;s subsidiaries, and the Company has granted to Intel and Intel&#146;s subsidiaries, non-exclusive, royalty-free
licenses all patents that are either owned or controlled by the parties that are either owned or controlled by the parties at any time that have a first effective filing date or priority date prior to the five-year anniversary of the effective date
of the Cross License Agreement (the &#147;Capture Period&#148;) to make, have made, use, sell, offer to sell, import and otherwise dispose of certain semiconductor- and electronic-related products anywhere in the world. Under the Cross License
Agreement, Intel has rights to make semiconductor products for third parties, but the third party product designs are not licensed as a result of such manufacture. The Company has rights to perform assembly and testing for third parties, but not
rights to make semiconductor products for third parties. </FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">The term of the Cross License Agreement continues until the
expiration of the last to expire of the licensed patents, unless earlier terminated. A party can terminate the Cross License Agreement or the rights and licenses of the other party if the other party materially breaches the Cross License Agreement
and does not correct the material breach within 60 days. Upon such termination the terminated party&#146;s license rights terminate but the terminating party&#146;s license rights continue, subject to that party&#146;s continued compliance with the
terms of the Cross License Agreement. The Cross License Agreement and the Capture Period will automatically terminate if a party undergoes a Change of Control (as defined in the Cross License Agreement), and both parties&#146; licenses will
terminate. Upon the bankruptcy of a party, that party may assume, but may not assign, the Cross License Agreement, and in the event that the Cross License Agreement cannot be assumed, the Cross License Agreement and the licenses granted will
terminate. </FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">As a result of the execution of the Cross License Agreement, a Reconciliation Event, as defined in the
Shareholders&#146; Agreement among the Company, Advanced Technology Investment Company LLC and GLOBALFOUNDRIES Inc. (&#147;<B><I>GF</I></B>&#148;) dated as of March&nbsp;2, 2009, has occurred. As a result, the Company&#146;s designees on the GF
board of directors will decrease from four to three. </FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">The foregoing descriptions of the Settlement Agreement and the Cross
License Agreement are qualified in their entirety by reference to the Settlement Agreement and the Cross License Agreement attached hereto as Exhibit 10.1 and Exhibit 10.2, which are each incorporated herein by reference. </FONT></P>

<TEXT>
<HTML><HEAD>
<TITLE>Settlement Agreement</TITLE>
</HEAD>
 <BODY BGCOLOR="WHITE">

 <P STYLE="margin-top:0px;margin-bottom:0px" ALIGN="right"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2"><B>Exhibit 10.1 </B></FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12px;margin-bottom:0px"
ALIGN="center"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2"><B><I>SETTLEMENT AGREEMENT BETWEEN </I></B></FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12px;margin-bottom:0px" ALIGN="center"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2"><B><I>ADVANCED
MICRO DEVICES INC. AND </I></B></FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12px;margin-bottom:0px" ALIGN="center"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2"><B><I>INTEL CORPORATION </I></B></FONT></P> <P
STYLE="margin-top:12px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%;padding-bottom:3px;"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">This Settlement Agreement (&#147;<U>Agreement</U>&#148;), effective as of the 11</FONT><FONT
STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="1"><SUP STYLE="vertical-align:baseline; position:relative; bottom:.8ex">th</SUP></FONT><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2"> day of November 2009 (<U>&#147;Effective Date</U>&#148;), is entered
into by and between Advanced Micro Devices, Inc. (<U>&#147;AMD</U>&#148;), and Intel Corporation (&#147;<U>Intel</U>&#148;). In consideration of the agreement set forth below, and for other good and valuable consideration, the receipt and
sufficiency of which is hereby acknowledged, AMD and Intel agree as follows: </FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:24px;margin-bottom:0px" ALIGN="center"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2"><B>RECITALS </B></FONT></P> <P
STYLE="margin-top:12px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">A. Intel and AMD are parties, claimants, complainants or interested parties to the following lawsuits, disputes and administrative actions
(collectively, &#147;<U>Actions</U>&#148;): </FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:6px;margin-bottom:0px; margin-left:4%;margin-right:2%; text-indent:4%"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">1. <U>Delaware Litigation</U>. AMD filed suit
against Intel under the caption <I>Advanced Micro Devices, Inc. and AMD International Sales&nbsp;&amp; Services, Ltd. v. Intel Corp. and Intel Kabushiki Kaisha</I>, in the United States District Court for the District of Delaware, Civil Action
No.&nbsp;05-441 (JJF), subsequently consolidated for the purpose of discovery as part of MDL No.&nbsp;05-1717,&nbsp;entitled <I>In re Intel Corporation Microprocessor Litigation</I>. This suit is referred to herein as the &#147;<U>Delaware
Litigation</U>&#148;; </FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:6px;margin-bottom:0px; margin-left:4%; text-indent:4%"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">2. <U>Japan Litigations</U>. Intel Kabushiki Kaisha, a Japanese Corporation and a wholly
owned subsidiary of Intel (&#147;<U>Intel IJKK</U>&#148;), is a defendant in two suits brought in Japanese courts by AMD Japan Ltd. (&#147;<U>AMD Japan</U>&#148;), a wholly-owned subsidiary of AMD. AMD Japan filed a suit in the Tokyo District Court
on June&nbsp;30, 2005, case number 2005 (Wa) 13151, alleging acts of unfair competition by Intel IJKK involving sales of microprocessors to Japanese manufacturers of personal computers. Claimed damages in the Tokyo District Court suit are $55
million and interest based on Section&nbsp;709 of the Civil Code of Japan. Also on June&nbsp;30, 2005, AMD Japan filed a suit in the Tokyo High Court, case number 2005 (Wa) 4, alleging violations of the Antimonopoly Law of Japan principally
involving sales of microprocessors to Japanese manufacturers of personal computers. Claimed damages in the Tokyo High Court suit are $50 million and interest based on Section&nbsp;25 of the Antimonopolization Law of Japan. These suits are referred
to herein as the &#147;<U>Japan Litigations</U>&#148;; </FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:6px;margin-bottom:0px; margin-left:4%; text-indent:4%"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">3. <U>GF Dispute</U>. On or about March&nbsp;9, 2009,
AMD closed a transaction with Advanced Technology Investment Company &#147;ATIC&#148; through which, among other actions, ATIC and AMD created a venture called GLOBALFOUNDRIES (&#147;<U>GF</U>&#148;), transferred AMD&#146;s wafer manufacturing
operations to GF, and claimed that GF was a subsidiary as defined under a January&nbsp;1,&nbsp;2001 Intel/AMD patent cross-license, and as such, entitled to rights thereunder. Intel claims that various aspects of this transaction have breached the
Intel/AMD patent cross-license. Intel also has advised AMD and GF
</FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:0px;margin-bottom:0px"><FONT SIZE="1">&nbsp;</FONT></P>
 <P STYLE="margin-top:0px;margin-bottom:0px" ALIGN="center"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">1 </FONT></P>


<p Style='page-break-before:always'>
<HR  SIZE="3" style="COLOR:#999999" WIDTH="100%" ALIGN="CENTER">

 <P STYLE="margin-top:0px;margin-bottom:0px; margin-left:4%"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">
that by using, manufacturing, selling, offering to sell and/or importing products utilizing Intel&#146;s patented inventions without a license, AMD and GF are each infringing certain of
Intel&#146;s patents. In response, AMD has accused Intel of breaching that patent cross-license. </FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">B. AMD has alleged various
claims against Intel in the Actions, including without limitation that Intel has been leveraging dominance in the supply of x86 microprocessors through the use of exclusionary, pricing, discount and other practices that unlawfully restrict
AMD&#146;s ability to compete. In the Delaware Litigation, both Intel and AMD have accused each other of violating the relevant standards related to evidence retention. </FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT
STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">C. Intel filed an answer in the Delaware Litigation on September&nbsp;1, 2005, denying all the material allegations of the complaint and asserting various defenses to each of AMD&#146;s claims. Intel
continues to dispute AMD&#146;s claims. </FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">D. To avoid the time and expense of litigation, and without any admission of
liability or fault, the Parties wish to fully, finally and forever resolve, compromise and settle the Actions on the terms and conditions set forth below. Further, Intel and AMD acknowledge that for most of the last three decades, their relationship
has been difficult, challenging and often acrimonious. Intel and AMD wish to change that relationship going forward, both by &#147;wiping the slate clean&#148; as to all past grievances, and by approaching future grievances in a constructive manner
designed to address and resolve such grievances amicably, if possible. </FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:24px;margin-bottom:0px" ALIGN="center"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2"><B>TERMS AND CONDITIONS </B></FONT></P> <P
STYLE="margin-top:12px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2"><B>1.0 <U>DEFINITIONS</U> </B></FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:6px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT
STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">1.1 &#147;<U>Actions</U>&#148; shall have the meaning set forth in Recital A. </FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman"
SIZE="2">1.2 &#147;<U>Administrative Action</U>&#148; shall mean and include without limitation (i)&nbsp;the Administrative Actions listed on <U>Exhibit D</U> attached hereto and incorporated by reference; (ii)&nbsp;any other investigation or
inquiry, of which AMD is aware, by any judicial or administrative authority in any jurisdiction concerning Intel&#146;s business practices and/or whether Intel has violated the competition, antitrust or unfair competition laws or regulations of any
jurisdiction; and (iii)&nbsp;any judicial or administrative action arising out of or seeking to review any action described in (i)&nbsp;or (ii)&nbsp;of this definition. </FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT
STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">1.3 &#147;<U>Administrative Complaint</U>&#148; shall mean any investigation or inquiry by a competition enforcement or regulatory body to which AMD has complained or petitioned regarding Intel&#146;s
business practices or in which AMD has submitted information or any white paper regarding such conduct, whether voluntarily or in response to a request by such body, at any time from January&nbsp;1, 1996 to the Effective Date, including without
limitation the Administrative Complaints listed on Exhibit D and attached hereto and incorporated by reference. </FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">1.4
&#147;<U>AMD</U>&#148; shall mean Advanced Micro Devices, Inc<I>. </I>In Section&nbsp;4.1 and 4.3, &#147;AMD&#148; shall also mean AMD International Sales and Services Ltd. (a Delaware corporation) (hereinafter &#147;AMD ISS&#148;). In
Section&nbsp;4.2, &#147;AMD&#148; shall also mean AMD ISS, and AMD&#146;s and AMD ISS&#146;s predecessors, successors, subsidiaries, affiliates and assigns, as well as the past and present officers, directors, employees, agents and attorneys of any
of them. </FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:0px;margin-bottom:0px"><FONT SIZE="1">&nbsp;</FONT></P>
 <P STYLE="margin-top:0px;margin-bottom:0px" ALIGN="center"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">2 </FONT></P>


<p Style='page-break-before:always'>
<HR  SIZE="3" style="COLOR:#999999" WIDTH="100%" ALIGN="CENTER">

 <P STYLE="margin-top:0px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">1.5 &#147;<U>AMD Microprocessor</U>&#148; shall have the same meaning as the term &#147;AMD
Processor&#148; in the New Patent Cross License. </FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">1.6 &#147;<U>Claims</U>&#148; shall mean and includes counterclaims and
cross-claims, as well as any and all actions, causes of action, claims, costs, damages, debts, demands, expenses, liabilities, losses, obligations, proceedings and suits of every kind and nature, liquidated or unliquidated, fixed or contingent, in
law, equity or otherwise, and whether presently known or unknown, including but not limited to all such matters that have been or could have been asserted in the Actions. </FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT
STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">1.7 &#147;<U>Computer Product</U>&#148; shall mean one or more Personal Computers that include one or more x86 Intel Microprocessors or x86 AMD Microprocessors. </FONT></P> <P
STYLE="margin-top:12px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">1.8 &#147;<U>Dispute</U>&#148; shall have the meaning set forth in Section&nbsp;7.1. </FONT></P> <P
STYLE="margin-top:12px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">1.9 &#147;<U>EC Litigation</U>&#148; shall mean Intel&#146;s Application for Annulment Pursuant to Articles 230 and 229 EC of Commission
Decision C(2009) 3726 final of 13&nbsp;May 2009 in Case COMP/C-3/37.990 Intel, filed with the Court of First Instance of the European Communities. </FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT
STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">1.10 &#147;<U>FTC Investigation</U>&#148; shall mean the U.S. Federal Trade Commission investigation In the Matter of Intel Corporation, No.&nbsp;061-0247. </FONT></P> <P
STYLE="margin-top:12px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">1.11 &#147;<U>Intel</U>&#148; shall mean Intel Corporation. In Section&nbsp;4.1, &#147;Intel&#148; shall also mean Intel IJKK, and
Intel&#146;s and Intel IJKK&#146;s predecessors, successors, subsidiaries, affiliates and assigns, as well as the past and present officers, directors, employees, agents and attorneys of any of them. In Sections 4.2 and 4.3, &#147;Intel&#148; shall
also mean Intel and Intel IJKK. </FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">1.12 &#147;<U>Intel Customers</U>&#148; shall mean any and all persons or entities that
purchase Intel Microprocessors or that distributes or sells Intel Microprocessors, platforms for Computer Products that include Intel Microprocessors, or Computer Products containing Intel Microprocessors. </FONT></P> <P
STYLE="margin-top:12px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">1.13 <U>&#147;Intel Microprocessor</U>&#148; shall have the same meaning as the term &#147;Intel Processor&#148; in the New Patent
Cross-License. </FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">1.14 &#147;<U>New York Litigation</U>&#148; shall mean the lawsuit filed against Intel by the State of New
York and as of the Effective Date styled <I>State of New York, by Attorney General Andrew M. Cuomo v. Intel Corporation</I>, Civil Action No.&nbsp;09-827 (UNA)., pending in the United States District Court for the District of Delaware. </FONT></P>
<P STYLE="margin-top:12px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">1.15 &#147;<U>Party</U>&#148; shall mean Intel or AMD. </FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT
STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">1.16 &#147;<U>Parties</U>&#148; shall mean AMD and Intel collectively. </FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">1.17
&#147;<U>Person</U>&#148; shall mean any natural person, and any corporation, partnership, limited liability company or other legal entity recognized in any jurisdiction in the world. </FONT></P> <P
STYLE="margin-top:12px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">1.18 &#147;<U>Personal Computer</U>&#148; shall mean one or more desktop (including without limitation servers and workstations), laptop,
tablet, netbook or notebook computers. For clarity, &#147;Personal Computer&#148; shall not mean smartphones, cell phones, Pocket PCs or consumer electronic devices. </FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:0px;margin-bottom:0px"><FONT
SIZE="1">&nbsp;</FONT></P>
 <P STYLE="margin-top:0px;margin-bottom:0px" ALIGN="center"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">3 </FONT></P>


<p Style='page-break-before:always'>
<HR  SIZE="3" style="COLOR:#999999" WIDTH="100%" ALIGN="CENTER">

 <P STYLE="margin-top:0px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">1.19 &#147;<U>GF Agreement</U>&#148; shall mean the Patent Agreement between Intel and GF,
of even date herewith, attached hereto as <U>Exhibit&nbsp;C</U> and incorporated herein by reference. </FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">1.20
&#147;<U>Post-Effective Date Conduct</U>&#148; shall mean conduct of Intel or AMD occurring after the Effective Date. </FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">1.21
&#147;<U>Pre-Effective Date Conduct</U>&#148; shall have the meaning set forth in Section&nbsp;4.7. </FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">1.22 &#147;<U>Specified
AMD Product</U>&#148; shall mean (i)&nbsp;an AMD Microprocessor, (ii)&nbsp;a Computer Product that contains one or more AMD Microprocessors, and/or (iii)&nbsp;a platform for a Computer Product that contains one or more AMD Microprocessors.
</FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">1.23 &#147;<U>Third Party</U>&#148; shall mean any Person other than AMD and Intel. </FONT></P> <P
STYLE="margin-top:12px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">1.24 &#147;<U>New Patent Cross License</U>&#148; shall mean the Patent Cross License between Intel and AMD, of even date herewith, attached
hereto as <U>Exhibit B</U> and incorporated herein by reference. </FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:18px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2"><B>2.0 <U>BUSINESS PRACTICES</U> </B></FONT></P> <P
STYLE="margin-top:6px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">As part of the overall resolution of the Actions, and without conceding expressly or by implication that any of the following business
practices are required by the laws of any jurisdiction, or any order issued by any regulatory or adjudicative body, anywhere in the world, or that contrary business practices would violate any such laws or orders, the Parties agree to adhere to the
Business Practices set forth in Section&nbsp;2.0 for the term, and subject to the conditions, set forth in Section&nbsp;6.0. </FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:18px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman"
SIZE="2">2.1 <U>SALES AND MARKETING PRACTICES: CUSTOMERS</U> </FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:6px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">2.1.1 Intel shall not award, offer, grant, pay or extend
(hereinafter, collectively, &#147;<U>Award</U>&#148;) any discount, rebate, or other financial or non-financial benefit, inducement or consideration of any kind (hereinafter, collectively, &#147;<U>Benefit</U>&#148;), that is conditioned orally, in
writing or through some other express or implied mutual understanding between Intel and the customer, on the customer limiting or restricting, or agreeing to be limited or restricted in, its freedom to satisfy any or all of its demand for
microprocessors for Computer Products through the customer&#146;s: </FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">a. exclusive use of x86 Intel Microprocessors, or
platforms for Computer Products that include Intel Microprocessors, in all of its business, or in any geography, market segment, product segment, or distribution channel; or </FONT></P> <P
STYLE="margin-top:12px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">b. limiting or delaying its purchase or use of Specified AMD Products on a geographic, platform, market segment, distribution channel,
volume, share of purchase, or any other basis. </FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">PROVIDED, HOWEVER, that nothing in this Section&nbsp;2.1.1 shall limit
Intel&#146;s right (i)&nbsp;to lawfully compete on the merits for selection by the customer for any then-current design award or for satisfaction of any or all of the customer&#146;s then-current demand for microprocessors in a manner consistent
with this Agreement or (ii)&nbsp;to condition a Benefit on the exclusive use of Intel microprocessors in a segment or channel where (a)&nbsp;Intel is making a significant, bona fide investment to enable the customer&#146;s entry into such segment or
channel, (b)&nbsp;the period of
</FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:0px;margin-bottom:0px"><FONT SIZE="1">&nbsp;</FONT></P>
 <P STYLE="margin-top:0px;margin-bottom:0px" ALIGN="center"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">4 </FONT></P>


<p Style='page-break-before:always'>
<HR  SIZE="3" style="COLOR:#999999" WIDTH="100%" ALIGN="CENTER">

 <P STYLE="margin-top:0px;margin-bottom:0px"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">
exclusivity is no longer than necessary for Intel to receive a reasonable commercial return on its customer-specific investment in such entry and (c)&nbsp;all of the terms and conditions of such
exclusive arrangement, including without limitation its duration and all related consideration, are set forth in a written instrument executed both by Intel and the customer. Nothing in this Section&nbsp;2.1.1 or any other term in this Agreement
shall be construed to constitute AMD&#146;s acquiescence in Intel&#146;s right to engage in any of the conduct described as contested in Section&nbsp;3.5, and AMD reserves all rights to seek redress for any such conduct occurring after the Effective
Date. </FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">2.1.2 Intel shall not Award any Benefit that is conditioned orally, in writing or through some express or implied
mutual understanding, on a customer&#146;s: </FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">a. limiting, depositioning or delaying its marketing, promotion, launch,
advertising, production, distribution, sale or branding of any product containing an AMD Microprocessor; or </FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">b. forbearance,
delay or abstention from participating in, or allowing its products to be featured or exhibited in, any AMD promotional activity, product launch, trade show exhibit, advertising, or public relations campaign. </FONT></P> <P
STYLE="margin-top:12px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">2.1.3 Intel shall not withhold or threaten to withhold any Benefit, nor impose or threaten to impose any penalty, on the basis of a
customer&#146;s refusal to accede to a condition precluded by, or to enter into an understanding prohibited by, Sections 2.1.1 or 2.1.2. </FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:18px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT
STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">2.2 <U>SALES AND MARKETING PRACTICES: RETAILERS, DISTRIBUTORS, ODMs AND END USERS</U> </FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:6px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman"
SIZE="2">2.2.1 Intel shall not Award any Benefit that is conditioned, orally, in writing, or through some other express or implied mutual understanding, on a non-customer Third Party (including without limitation any retailer, distributor, original
design manufacturer (<U>&#147;ODM</U>&#148;), original equipment manufacturer (<U>&#147;OEM</U>&#148;), system integrator and value added reseller, but excluding any direct customer and any end user (hereinafter, collectively, <U>&#147;Non-Customer
Third Party</U>&#148;) limiting or restricting, or agreeing to be limited or restricted in, the Non-Customer Third Party&#146;s freedom to satisfy any or all of its demand for microprocessors for Computer Products through the Non-Customer Third
Party&#146;s: </FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">a. exclusive use of Intel Microprocessors, platforms for Computer Products that include Intel Microprocessors,
or Computer Products containing Intel Microprocessors in all of its business, or in any geography, market segment, product segment, outlet or distribution channel; or </FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT
STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">b. limiting, depositioning or delaying its purchase or use of Specified AMD Products on a geographic, platform, market segment, distribution channel, volume, share of purchase, or any other basis.
</FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">PROVIDED, HOWEVER, that nothing in this Section&nbsp;2.2.1 shall limit Intel&#146;s right (i)&nbsp;to compete on the merits
for selection by a Non-Customer Third Party for any design win or for satisfaction of any of Non-Customer&#146;s Third Party&#146;s then-current demand for microprocessors or (ii)&nbsp;to condition such a Benefit on the exclusive use of Intel
Microprocessors in a segment or channel where (a)&nbsp;Intel is making a significant, bona fide investment to enable the Non-Customer Third Party&#146;s entry into a segment or channel, (b)&nbsp;the period of exclusivity is no longer than reasonably
necessary for Intel to receive a reasonable commercial return on its Non-Customer Third Party-specific investment in such entry and (c)&nbsp;all of the terms and conditions of such exclusive arrangement, including without limitation its duration and
all related consideration, are set forth in a written instrument executed both by Intel and the Non-Customer Third Party. </FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:0px;margin-bottom:0px"><FONT SIZE="1">&nbsp;</FONT></P>
 <P STYLE="margin-top:0px;margin-bottom:0px" ALIGN="center"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">5 </FONT></P>

 <P STYLE="margin-top:0px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">2.2.2 Intel shall not Award any Benefit that is conditioned orally, in writing or through
some other express or implied mutual understanding, on a Non-Customer Third Party&#146;s: </FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">a. limiting, depositioning or
delaying its purchase, sale, design, development, marketing, promotion, launch, production, distribution, branding, advertising, exhibition, offer or display of any Specified AMD Product; or </FONT></P> <P
STYLE="margin-top:12px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">b. forbearance, delay or abstention from participating in, or allowing itself or its products to be featured or exhibited in, any AMD
promotional activity, product launch, trade show exhibit, advertising or public relations campaign. </FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">2.2.3 Intel shall not
withhold or threaten to withhold any Benefit on the basis of a Third Party&#146;s refusal to accede to a condition precluded by, or enter into an understanding prohibited by, Sections 2.2.1 and 2.2.2. </FONT></P> <P
STYLE="margin-top:18px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">2.3 <U>TECHNICAL PRACTICES</U> </FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:6px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT
STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">Intel shall not include any Artificial Performance Impairment in any Intel product or require any Third Party to include an Artificial Performance Impairment in the Third Party&#146;s product. As used in
this Section&nbsp;2.3, &#147;<U>Artificial Performance Impairment</U>&#148; means an affirmative engineering or design action by Intel (but not a failure to act) that (i)&nbsp;degrades the performance or operation of a Specified AMD product,
(ii)&nbsp;is not a consequence of an Intel Product Benefit and (iii)&nbsp;is made intentionally to degrade the performance or operation of a Specified AMD Product. For purposes of this Section&nbsp;2.3, &#147;<U>Product Benefit</U>&#148; shall mean
any benefit, advantage, or improvement in terms of performance, operation, price, cost, manufacturability, reliability, compatibility, or ability to operate or enhance the operation of another product. </FONT></P> <P
STYLE="margin-top:12px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">In no circumstances shall this Section&nbsp;2.3 impose or be construed to impose any obligation on Intel to (i)&nbsp;take any act that would
provide a Product Benefit to any AMD or other non-Intel product, either when such AMD or non-Intel product is used alone or in combination with any other product, (ii)&nbsp;optimize any products for Specified AMD Products, or (iii)&nbsp;provide any
technical information, documents, or know how to AMD. </FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:18px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">2.4 <U>PRACTICES NOT A BREACH OF THIS AGREEMENT</U> </FONT></P> <P
STYLE="margin-top:6px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">2.4.1 Notwithstanding any of other provision in this Agreement, in no event shall a Third Party&#146;s action, or forbearance from taking an
action, merely as a consequence of Intel securing business sought by AMD constitute a breach of this Agreement by Intel, so long as Intel does not violate any of the terms of Sections 2.1.1 through 2.3. </FONT></P> <P
STYLE="margin-top:12px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">2.4.2 Nothing in this Agreement shall prohibit Intel in connection with its offer or sale of any microprocessor from Awarding any Benefit or
Discount to a Third Party where such offer or sale, or term or condition thereof, meets in good faith a respective Benefit, Discount, or a type of term and/or condition then being offered to that Third Party by any other supplier with respect to a
reasonably comparable microprocessor. </FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:0px;margin-bottom:0px"><FONT SIZE="1">&nbsp;</FONT></P>
 <P STYLE="margin-top:0px;margin-bottom:0px" ALIGN="center"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">6 </FONT></P>

 <P STYLE="margin-top:0px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">2.4.3 Intel may, without violating this Agreement, limit a Third Party&#146;s use of its
market development funds or other promotional funds to the promotion of Intel and Intel-based products unless such limitation includes a Restriction. <U>&#147;Restriction</U>&#148; shall mean a requirement imposed or required by or on behalf of
Intel that the recipient of market development funds or other promotional funds from Intel (&#147;Recipient&#148;) may not include AMD or AMD products in a portion of an OEM catalog, retailer circular, web page or site, point-of-sale marketing
piece, or event where such respective portion is not funded by Intel. The Intel Inside program rules dated as of April&nbsp;1, 2009 are conclusively presumed permissible under this Agreement, except to the extent they include a Restriction.
</FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:18px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">2.5 <U>PROCUREMENT</U> </FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:6px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT
STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">2.5.1 Neither Intel nor AMD shall either request of, suggest to or cause any public procurement agent or authority to issue or adopt a procurement specification for a Computer Product that excludes the
qualification of a Computer Product employing the other Party&#146;s microprocessor on the basis of a function, industry standard specification or benchmark that is immaterial to the intended use of the product. </FONT></P> <P
STYLE="margin-top:18px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">2.6 <U>MISCELLANEOUS PROVISIONS </U> </FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:6px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT
STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">2.6.1 Intel shall conduct regular training of all of its personnel engaged in microprocessor sales and marketing concerning the requirements and prohibitions contained in the Business Practices Section of
this Agreement. </FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">2.6.2 Intel shall provide a copy of Section&nbsp;2.0 of this Agreement to its customers by making it
reasonably available on its website in a conspicuous manner. </FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2"><B>3.0 <U>EXECUTION OF OTHER CONTEMPORANEOUS AGREEMENTS AND
DISMISSAL OF THE ACTIONS</U> </B></FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">3.1 <U>Consideration</U>. The Parties agree that the covenants, promises and releases set
forth herein, in the New Patent Cross License, and in the GF Agreement, constitute fair and adequate consideration for the Parties&#146; execution of this Agreement and the agreements set forth in Section&nbsp;3.7. </FONT></P> <P
STYLE="margin-top:12px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">a. As consideration for this settlement agreement alone, Intel agrees to pay and shall pay to AMD the total sum of One Billion, Two Hundred
and Fifty Million Dollars ($1,250,000,000), in U.S. currency, within thirty (30)&nbsp;days of the Effective Date. </FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">b. This
payment shall be non-refundable and shall be made by one or more wire transfers of immediately available funds to the following AMD account: </FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:6px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT

 <P STYLE="margin-top:0px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">3.2 <U>No Admission of Liability</U>. The Parties agree that the settlement of the Actions
is intended solely as a compromise of disputed Claims. Each Party expressly denies any liability or wrongdoing. A Party&#146;s execution of this Agreement, the agreements set forth in Section&nbsp;3.7 below, its agreement to any term of this
Agreement and/or the agreements set forth in Section&nbsp;3.7 below, and any action taken by a Party pursuant to this Agreement and/or the agreements set forth in Section&nbsp;3.7 below do not constitute and shall not be construed as an admission of
liability or as a concession by any Party that any Claim or defense asserted or that could have been asserted by the other Party is valid. </FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT
STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">3.3 <U>Dismissal and Withdrawal of Claims and Allegations</U>. Immediately after execution of this Agreement by all Parties, the Parties shall cause their respective counsel to sign Stipulations for
Dismissal with Prejudice of the Delaware Litigation and the Japan Litigations, with each Party to bear its own attorneys&#146; fees and costs, including without limitation proposed Orders of Dismissal, in the forms attached hereto as <U>Exhibits A-1
and A-2</U> and incorporated herein by reference (&#147;<U>Stipulations of Dismissal with Prejudice and Proposed Orders</U>&#148;). AMD shall immediately file and/or lodge the Stipulations of Dismissal with Prejudice and Proposed Orders to:
(i)&nbsp;effect dismissal of the Actions with prejudice, including without limitation all Claims asserted therein, with each Party to bear its own attorneys&#146; fees and costs; and (ii)&nbsp;authorize the Court in the Delaware Litigation to retain
jurisdiction to enforce this Agreement to the limited extent permitted by Sections 3.6.a and 7, below. </FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:18px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">3.4 <U>Administrative
Complaints and Administrative Actions</U>. </FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:6px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">a. AMD represents and warrants that it has not initiated, filed, intervened in or
attempted to intervene in any Administrative Complaint or Administrative Action concerning Intel except for those listed on Exhibit D. </FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman"
SIZE="2">b. For each and every Administrative Complaint filed by AMD, AMD agrees to promptly (and in no event later than five (5)&nbsp;days after the Effective Date) notify in writing each authority to which such complaint was made that except as
provided in Section&nbsp;3.5, AMD has resolved its disagreements with and complaints concerning Intel contained in that Administrative Complaint and believes that this Agreement provides AMD with fair compensation for any and all actual or alleged
harm and damages that AMD did or may have suffered in connection with matters addressed or encompassed within such Administrative Complaint. </FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT
STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">c. For each and every Administrative Action listed on Exhibit D, AMD agrees to promptly (and in no event later than five (5)&nbsp;days after the Effective Date) notify in writing each authority listed on
Exhibit D that except as provided in Section&nbsp;3.5, AMD has resolved its disagreements with and complaints concerning Intel contained in that Administrative Complaint and believes that this Agreement provides AMD with fair compensation for any
and all actual or alleged harm and damages that AMD did or may have suffered in connection with matters discussed in the Administrative Complaint. In addition, AMD agrees that it will not ghost-write or edit any other briefs, pleadings, or
&#147;friend of the court&#148; or &#147;friend of the tribunal&#148; materials or briefs in any Administrative Action. </FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">d.
Except as provided in Section&nbsp;3.5, AMD agrees not to participate in any Administrative Action that concerns, discusses or relates to conduct, events or allegations that occurred prior to the Effective Date, except as may be explicitly required
by applicable law, such as responding to a lawful subpoena or other process administered in that Action. AMD agrees that it will not seek to intervene as a party or for any other status in any Administrative Action, and will promptly (and in no
event later than five (5)&nbsp;days after the Effective Date) withdraw any
</FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:0px;margin-bottom:0px"><FONT SIZE="1">&nbsp;</FONT></P>
 <P STYLE="margin-top:0px;margin-bottom:0px" ALIGN="center"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">8 </FONT></P>


<p Style='page-break-before:always'>
<HR  SIZE="3" style="COLOR:#999999" WIDTH="100%" ALIGN="CENTER">

 <P STYLE="margin-top:0px;margin-bottom:0px"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">
motion to intervene as a party or for any other status in any Administrative Action, including without limitation any appeals from a denial of such a motion to intervene. For clarity,
Section&nbsp;3.4.d is not intended to and shall not limit AMD in any way from exercising any lawful right to petition any government agency regarding any conduct or event occurring after the Effective Date, nor shall it limit AMD&#146;s rights as
provided in Section&nbsp;3.5. </FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:18px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">3.5 <U>Other Issues Related to Administrative Actions.</U> </FONT></P> <P
STYLE="margin-top:6px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">a. AMD has challenged as illegal certain alleged Intel pricing practices for Intel Microprocessors and platforms for use in Computer Products
by which a price, discount, rebate or other financial benefit (&#147;<U>Discount</U>&#148;) was conditioned on a customer&#146;s volume or share of such Intel microprocessor purchases from Intel and on the customer&#146;s achieving the qualifying
threshold, and was then applied to purchases below the volume level qualifying the customer for the Discount (&#147;<U>Retroactive Discount</U>&#148;). </FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT
STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">b. AMD has challenged as illegal certain alleged Intel pricing practices by which a fund for defraying the cost of Intel Microprocessors for use in Computer Products was established to allow a customer to
make bids such that, after accounting for the use of such fund, the cost of the Intel Microprocessor would be below the appropriate measure of variable cost (&#147;<U>Accused Bid Bucket</U>&#148;). </FONT></P> <P
STYLE="margin-top:12px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">c. AMD has challenged as illegal certain alleged Intel pricing practices by which a Discount was provided to an end user purchaser of any
Computer Product containing an Intel Microprocessor that when combined with any other Discount provided by Intel with respect to the Intel Microprocessor included in the purchased Computer Product, results in an effective Intel Microprocessor price
below the appropriate measure of variable cost (&#147;<U>Accused End-User Discount</U>&#148;). </FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">d. Intel and AMD do not have
any understanding or agreement regarding Intel&#146;s freedom to enter into Retroactive Discounts, Accused Bid Buckets and/or Accused End-User Discounts and this Agreement in no way affects or limits Intel&#146;s decisions regarding pricing and
discounting or restricts or limits in any way Intel&#146;s complete freedom in setting prices and discounts and in devising and implementing any and all related policies and procedures. </FONT></P> <P
STYLE="margin-top:12px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">e. Intel agrees that in the event it enters into voluntary settlement discussions with a government authority in the EC Litigation, New York
Litigation or the FTC Investigation, and if such government authority proposes to include in a consent judgment or other governmental order a prohibition against Retroactive Discounts, Accused Bid Buckets or Accused End-User Discounts, Intel will
not challenge such a prohibition as a general matter, although it may challenge the scope or specific language of the prohibition. It is expressly understood, however, that Intel remains completely free to litigate and oppose the EC Litigation, New
York Litigation and the FTC Investigation to finality and to reject any proposed settlement. </FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">f. Intel understands that AMD
will contend to the European Commission, the New York Attorney General and to the U.S. Federal Trade Commission that any Intel Retroactive Discounts, Accused Bid Buckets or Accused End-User Discounts are anticompetitive and unlawful and that they
should be prohibited by any consent order or remedial injunction entered in the EC Litigation, New York Litigation and/or the FTC Investigation. In the course of contending for the prohibition of Intel Retroactive Discounts, Accused Bid Buckets or
Accused End-User Discounts in accordance with this Section&nbsp;3.5.d, AMD may disclose to the respective authorities the terms of Sections 2.0 and 3.5 hereto. </FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:0px;margin-bottom:0px"><FONT SIZE="1">&nbsp;</FONT></P>
 <P STYLE="margin-top:0px;margin-bottom:0px" ALIGN="center"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">9 </FONT></P>


<p Style='page-break-before:always'>
<HR  SIZE="3" style="COLOR:#999999" WIDTH="100%" ALIGN="CENTER">

 <P STYLE="margin-top:0px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">3.6 <U>Further Provisions Regarding Existing Litigations and Related Matters</U>.
</FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:6px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">a. <U>Delaware Litigation and Japan Litigations</U>. Unless otherwise required by law, the Parties shall follow the terms of
the Confidentiality Agreement and Protective Order entered September&nbsp;26, 2006 in the Delaware Litigation, except that the first sentence of Section&nbsp;24 of that Order shall be modified to substitute the portion of the sentence reading
&#147;Within one hundred twenty (120)&nbsp;days of the conclusion of the AMD Litigation or the Class Litigation&#148; to instead read, &#147;Within sixty (60)&nbsp;days of the conclusion of the AMD Litigation, the Class Litigation or any other
lawsuit, Administrative Complaint or Administrative Action that alleges that Intel has violated the antitrust, unfair competition or similar laws or regulations of any jurisdiction.&#148; Each AMD or Intel outside counsel who maintains access to
Protective Order Material shall maintain such material in a manner strictly subject to the terms of that protective order, as modified, and the Court&#146;s continuing jurisdiction to enforce the terms thereof and redress violations thereof, except
that, notwithstanding any term of the Protective Order to the contrary, Intel shall have the right to provide such material to any investigating agency, court or other tribunal, provided that it seeks from such agency, court, or other tribunal
protection against the public disclosure of such material to the maximum extent provided for under the rules of such agency, court, or tribunal. Nothing in this Paragraph 3.6(a) shall restrict either party&#146;s rights under Paragraph 16 of the
referenced Confidentiality Agreement and Protective Order in the Delaware litigation. </FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">b. <U>GF Dispute</U>. Intel and AMD
hereby withdraw all Claims they have threatened to assert, or have asserted, in the GF Dispute. </FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:18px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">3.7 <U>Execution of
Contemporaneous Agreements</U>. </FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:6px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">a. Intel and AMD shall enter into the New Patent Cross License, and the entry into such
agreement is a condition precedent to the effectiveness of this Agreement. </FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">b. Intel and GF shall enter into the GF Agreement,
and the entry into such agreement is a condition precedent to the effectiveness of this Agreement. </FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:18px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2"><B>4.0 <U>MUTUAL RELEASES
AND COVENANTS NOT TO SUE</U> </B></FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:6px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">4.1 <U>AMD Release</U>. Except for the rights and obligations expressly created or reserved
by this Agreement and by the agreements referred to in Section&nbsp;3.7, AMD does hereby irrevocably release, acquit and forever discharge Intel from any and all Claims that AMD ever had, now has or hereafter may acquire against Intel, whether known
or unknown, on account of any action, inaction, matter, thing or event, that occurred or failed to occur at any time in the past, from the beginning of time through and including the Effective Date, including, without limitation, any and all Claims
based on or arising out of, in whole or in part, the Actions or the facts underlying the Actions and any claims that could have been raised in the Actions up to the Effective Date. All third parties included within the scope of the preceding
release, pursuant to Section&nbsp;1.10, are expressly agreed to be third-party beneficiaries of this Agreement. </FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">4.2 <U>Intel
Release</U>. Except for the rights and obligations expressly created or reserved by this Agreement and by the agreements described in Section&nbsp;3.7, Intel does hereby irrevocably release, acquit and forever discharge AMD, GF and ATIC from any and
all Claims that Intel ever had, now has or hereafter may acquire against AMD, GF and ATIC, whether known or unknown, on account of any action, inaction, matter, thing or event, that occurred or failed to occur at any time in the past, from the
beginning of time through and including the Effective
</FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:0px;margin-bottom:0px"><FONT SIZE="1">&nbsp;</FONT></P>

Date, including, without limitation, any and all Claims based on or arising out of, in whole or in part, the Actions or the facts underlying the Actions and any claims that could have been raised
in the Actions up to the Effective Date. All third parties included within the scope of the preceding release, pursuant to Section&nbsp;1.4, are expressly agreed to be third-party beneficiaries of this Agreement. </FONT></P> <P
STYLE="margin-top:12px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">4.3 <U>AMD Release of Intel Customers</U>. AMD does hereby irrevocably release, acquit and forever discharge Intel Customers from any and
all Claims that AMD ever had, now has or hereafter may acquire against Intel Customers, based on their actions or inactions prior to the Effective Date that were or could have been alleged to further any aspect of Intel&#146;s allegedly wrongful
conduct that was alleged or could have been raised in the Actions. All third parties included within the scope of the preceding release, pursuant to Section&nbsp;1.11, are expressly agreed to be third-party beneficiaries of this Agreement.
</FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">4.4 <U>Complete Settlement</U>. Except for the rights and obligations expressly created or reserved by this Agreement and by
the agreements referred to in Section&nbsp;3.7, each Party does hereby release, acquit, and forever discharge the other Parties from all Claims for attorneys&#146; fees, contribution, costs, expenses and/or indemnity based on or arising out of, in
whole or in part, the Actions. The Parties agree that this Agreement is in full and complete settlement of the rights and obligations of the Parties in connection with the Actions. </FONT></P> <P
STYLE="margin-top:12px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">4.5 <U>Waiver of Statutory Provisions</U>. The Parties expressly waive the benefits of any statutory provision or common law rule that
provides, in sum or substance, that a release does not extend to claims that the Party does not know or expect to exist in its favor at the time of executing the release, which if known by it, would have materially affected its settlement with the
other Party. In particular, but without limitation, each Party expressly waives the provisions of California Civil Code &#167; 1542, which reads: </FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT
STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2"><B>A GENERAL RELEASE DOES NOT EXTEND TO CLAIMS WHICH THE CREDITOR DOES NOT KNOW OR SUSPECT TO EXIST IN HIS FAVOR AT THE TIME OF EXECUTING THE RELEASE, WHICH IF KNOWN BY HIM MUST HAVE MATERIALLY AFFECTED
HIS SETTLEMENT WITH THE DEBTOR. </B></FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">4.6 <U>Covenant Not to Sue</U>. The Parties expressly understand that both direct and
indirect breaches of this Agreement are precluded. Therefore, the Parties each agree that they will not institute or prosecute, against the other, any action or other proceeding based in whole or in part upon any Claims released by this Agreement.
Further, the Parties each agree that they will not authorize, or solicit the commencement or prosecution against the other Party of, any action or other legal proceeding based in whole or in part upon any Claims released by this Agreement.
</FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">4.7. <U>Past Intel Conduct.</U> AMD agrees that, except as provided in Section&nbsp;3.5, this Agreement is a full, final and
fair resolution of any and all actual, alleged, claimed or potential harm suffered by AMD or that AMD has claimed or could claim to have incurred as a result of any Intel conduct or acts that occurred on or prior to the Effective Date, including
without limitation any such conduct complained of in the Actions (collectively <U>&#147;Pre-Effective Date Conduct</U>&#148;). AMD covenants and agrees that this Agreement fully compensates AMD for any and all such harm from Pre-Effective Date
Conduct, and accordingly, AMD agrees that, except as provided in Section&nbsp;3.5, it shall make no complaints to Governmental Authorities regarding any such conduct or harm. </FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:0px;margin-bottom:0px"><FONT

 <P STYLE="margin-top:0px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">4.8 <U>No Assignment of Claims</U>. Each Party hereto represents and warrants that it is the
sole and lawful owner of all right, title and interest in and to every Claim and every matter which it purports to release, and that it has not sold, assigned, transferred, hypothecated, pledged or encumbered, or otherwise disposed of, in whole or
in part, voluntarily or involuntarily, any Claims released pursuant to this Agreement. </FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:18px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2"><B>5.0 <U>CONFIDENTIALITY </U> </B>
</FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:6px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">5.1 <U>Confidentiality</U>. Sections 2.0 and 3.1.a of this Agreement shall not be confidential. The Parties shall keep every
other term of this Agreement confidential and shall not now or hereafter divulge these terms to any third Person except: </FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">a.
as provided in Sections 3.5.d and 5.2 of this Agreement; or </FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">b. with the prior written consent of the other Party; or
</FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">c. to any governmental body having jurisdiction to call therefore, including to the United States Securities and Exchange
Commission; or </FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">d. subject to 5.1.e, as otherwise may be required by law or legal process, including to legal and financial
advisors in their capacity of advising a party in such matters provided that any such legal and financial advisors first agree to maintain the Agreement in confidence; or </FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT
STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">e. during the course of litigation, so long as the disclosure of such terms and conditions are restricted in the same manner as is the confidential information of other litigating parties and so long as
(i)&nbsp;the restrictions are embodied in a court-entered Protective Order limiting disclosure and (ii)&nbsp;the disclosing Party informs the other Party in writing at least ten (10)&nbsp;days in advance of the disclosure; or </FONT></P> <P
STYLE="margin-top:12px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">f. in confidence to legal counsel, accountants, banks and financing sources and their advisors solely in connection with complying with
applicable law or undertaking financial transactions provided that any such legal counsel, accountants, banks and financial sources and their advisors first agree to maintain the Agreement in confidence. </FONT></P> <P
STYLE="margin-top:12px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">5.2. The Parties shall issue a press release in the form attached as Exhibit E attached hereto and incorporated by reference promptly after
all Parties have executed this Agreement and the agreements referenced in Section&nbsp;3.7. </FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">5.3 <U>Other Uses of this
Agreement</U>. This Agreement shall not be offered, admitted or admissible in any action, suit or proceeding as evidence or as an admission of any claim or liability; PROVIDED, HOWEVER<I>,</I> that any Party to this Agreement may use all or part of
this Agreement in a proceeding between the Parties to the extent necessary to enforce any right conferred on that Party by this Agreement. Any filing of or references to this Agreement or the provisions thereof in such a proceeding, with the
exception of Section&nbsp;2.0 shall be done under seal. </FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:12px;margin-bottom:0px; text-indent:4%"><FONT STYLE="font-family:Times New Roman" SIZE="2">5.4 Notwithstanding any other term in this Agreement, Intel may
provide at its election redacted or unredacted copies of this Agreement to respond to lawsuits, administrative proceedings or regulatory inquiries relating to the business practices at issue in any Administrative Complaint or Administrative Action
and any related issues. </FONT></P> <P STYLE="margin-top:0px;margin-bottom:0px"><FONT SIZE="1">&nbsp;</FONT></P>
